```text
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/docs/paper/experiments.tex:142:On Qwen, MCKP (11.23) beats the L0-random median (11.52) by 0.29 PPL and the naive positional heuristic (12.23) by 1.00 PPL, confirming that profiling-guided selection outperforms both random and depth-based assignment.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/docs/paper/experiments.tex:185:On Qwen2.5-7B, C4-calibrated MCKP achieves 11.29 PPL on WikiText-2 (vs.\ 15.45 uniform, a 27\% reduction), only 0.06 PPL above the WikiText-2-calibrated result (11.23).
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/docs/paper/conclusion.tex:4:A single constraint---limiting simultaneously active 2-bit heads to $k \leq 8$ (7\% of heads)---reduces perplexity on Qwen2.5-7B from 15.45 (uniform 4-bit) to 11.23, a 27.3\% improvement at $4\times$ compression.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/docs/paper/method.tex:56:The curve is U-shaped: at $k{=}0$ (uniform 4-bit), PPL is 15.45; allowing a few 2-bit heads reduces PPL by freeing budget for 8-bit heads on sensitive positions, reaching a minimum of 11.23 at $k{=}8$; beyond this point, compounding drives PPL upward, reaching 35.35 at $k{=}75$.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/docs/paper/method.tex:73:On Qwen2.5-7B, the profiler assigns very high distortion to Layer-0 heads at 2-bit: L0H0 increases PPL by 31.9\% relative to the bf16 baseline (9.81 $\to$ 12.94), L0H2 by 23.2\% (9.81 $\to$ 12.09), L0H3 by 11.7\% (9.81 $\to$ 10.96).
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/docs/paper/method.tex:83:It also explains the gap between constrained and unconstrained solvers in Table~\ref{tab:solver_equiv}: constrained greedy operates inside the additive regime and attains 11.23 PPL, matching the additive estimate $\sum_h d_h(b_h) + \text{baseline} \approx 11$; unconstrained solvers extrapolate the same additive model outside its valid range and miss true PPL by $3\text{--}4\times$ (32.45 for greedy and 36.92 for MCKP, against the $\approx$11 they would predict).
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/docs/paper/method.tex:100:Greedy & $k{\leq}8$ & 11.23 & 8 & 100 & 4 \\
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/docs/paper/method.tex:101:MCKP & $k{\leq}8$ & 11.23 & 8 & 100 & 4 \\
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/docs/paper/method.tex:106:\footnotetext{Standard-resolution profiling; hires profiling (64 seq $\times$ 2048 tok) achieves 10.91 (Appendix~\ref{sec:profiling_resolution}).}
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/docs/paper/method.tex:109:Table~\ref{tab:solver_equiv} shows that constrained greedy and constrained MCKP achieve the same PPL (11.23) with the same bit distribution on Qwen2.5-7B.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/docs/paper/abstract.tex:4:On Qwen2.5-7B at $4\times$ compression, constrained allocation achieves 11.23 perplexity, a 27.3\% reduction over uniform 4-bit.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/docs/paper/appendix.tex:16:High-resolution profiling improves constrained MCKP from 11.23 to 10.91 PPL ($-$0.32 absolute).
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/docs/paper/appendix.tex:25:Under hires profiling on Qwen2.5-7B, constrained MCKP yields mean PPL $10.99 \pm 0.06$ across 3 random calibration seeds (individual runs: 11.02, 10.90, 11.05), with std/mean below 0.6\%.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/docs/paper/appendix.tex:30:Float32 profiling (64 $\times$ 2048 tokens) yields an identical constrained allocation and PPL within seed variance (10.89 vs.\ 10.91, $\Delta = 0.02 < \sigma = 0.06$), confirming that bfloat16 precision is sufficient for the profiling step.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/docs/paper/appendix.tex:43:Joint allocation achieves 10.91 PPL; separated K/V allocation achieves 11.22 ($+$0.31 worse).

/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/docs/paper/introduction.tex:49:\item We identify 2-bit distortion compounding as the cause of catastrophic failure in unconstrained per-head KV cache quantization on hardware-aligned bit-widths, and show that a cardinality constraint on 2-bit assignments is a minimal sufficient fix, reducing Qwen2.5-7B PPL from 32--37 (unconstrained) to 11.23 (constrained) at $4\times$ compression (\S\ref{sec:compounding}, \S\ref{sec:experiments}).
```

```text
45	\begin{tabular}{lcccccc}
46	\toprule
47	Model & Baseline & Uniform & \multicolumn{2}{c}{Unconstrained} & Ours \\
48	 & (bf16) & (4-bit) & Greedy & MCKP & (Constr.) \\
49	\midrule
50	Qwen2.5-7B      & 9.81  & 15.45 & 32.45 & 36.92 & \textbf{11.23}$\pm$0.06 \\
51	Llama-3.1-8B    & 8.09  & 8.47  & ---   & ---   & \textbf{8.45}$^\dagger$ \\
52	Mistral-7B-v0.3 & 7.04  & 7.13  & ---   & ---   & 7.16$^{\dagger\ddagger}$ \\
53	Yi-1.5-9B$^\dagger$       & 7.02  & 7.22  & ---   & ---   & 7.22 \\
54	Gemma-2-9B      & 8.90  & 9.03  & ---   & ---   & 9.05 \\
55	\bottomrule
56	\end{tabular}
57	\end{table}
58	
59	Table~\ref{tab:main_results} summarizes the main results.
60	On Qwen2.5-7B, constrained MCKP achieves 11.23 PPL, a 27.3\% reduction over uniform 4-bit (15.45).
61	Higher-resolution profiling (64 seq $\times$ 2048 tok) further improves this to 10.91; we report the standard-profiling result here and analyze the effect of profiling resolution in Appendix~\ref{sec:profiling_resolution}.
62	Without the cardinality constraint, both greedy (32.45) and MCKP (36.92) perform \emph{worse} than uniform---a catastrophic failure caused by excessive 2-bit assignments that compound across layers.
63	The constraint eliminates this failure mode entirely.
64	Across 3 calibration seeds, constrained MCKP yields mean PPL 10.99$\pm$0.06 (std is 1.3\% of the uniform gap), with 90.2\% three-way allocation agreement, confirming profiling stability.
65	
66	On Llama-3.1-8B, the result is essentially indistinguishable from uniform (8.45 vs.\ 8.47, $\Delta < 0.02$ PPL).
67	Hires profiling reveals a spike pattern (concentration 0.57, $t_1/t_5{=}0.85$) dominated by a single top head (L2H5), leaving little cross-bitwidth gradient for non-uniform allocation to exploit.
68	
69	On Mistral-7B-v0.3, constrained MCKP (7.16, $k{=}4$) is slightly worse than uniform (7.13, $+$0.4\%).
70	This is a genuine negative result: the profiler reports near-zero sensitivity differences across heads, meaning the optimization has no reliable signal to exploit.
71	Mistral's sliding-window attention may contribute to this uniformity by limiting head-level variation in effective context length.
72	
73	On Yi-1.5-9B$^\dagger$, constrained MCKP ($k{=}2$) achieves 7.22 PPL, essentially identical to uniform 7.22 ($<$0.01\% difference).
74	Despite having the highest GQA ratio in our evaluation (8:1) and the highest concentration tested (0.71), Yi-1.5's sensitivity is dominated by a single extreme outlier (Layer-4 Head-3, $t_1/t_5{=}0.90$), a spike pattern that leaves no cross-bitwidth gradient for the solver to exploit.
```

D001\\\\n- id: D004\\\\n  date: 2026-05-13 21:55 UTC\\\\n  summary: 1.5B sanity 暴露 attention MSE 与 PPL 脱钩；7B 继续跑但需准备 metric 修正方案\\\\n  rationale: Qwen2.5-1.5B sanity 结果显示：(1) crossover 只在 8x 极端压缩出现（89.3% heads），此时 PPL\\\\n    已崩溃（14000+）；(2) 在实用的 4x 压缩下 full MCKP 反而比 quant-only 差 2.77 PPL，甚至比 uniform 4-bit\\\\n    差 3.41 PPL；(3) 根因是 attention output MSE 系统性低估 eviction 的 downstream 损伤——eviction\\\\n    是 structured error（完整丢弃 token），量化是 unstructured error（均匀噪声），MSE 无法区分两者的级联影响。形式上\\\\n    MVP gate PASS 但实质不成立（在 catastrophic regime 的 false positive）。7B 实验已提交继续跑，确认 pattern\\\\n    是否 model-agnostic。同时需准备 metric 修正：(a) layer-output MSE 替代 attention-output MSE；(b)\\\\n    eviction distortion penalty；(c) gate criteria 收紧排除 PPL>2x baseline 的 regime。\\\\n  related_experiments:\\\\n  - <details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

````text
Now I have a clearer picture of the `11.23±0.06` issue. Let me analyze it carefully:

**Table 1 (experiments.tex line 50)**:
```
Qwen2.5-7B & 9.81 & 15.45 & 32.45 & 36.92 & \textbf{11.23}$\pm$0.06
```

**Experiments text (line 64)**:
```
Across 3 calibration seeds, constrained MCKP yields mean PPL 10.99$\pm$0.06


```

So the table reports `11.23±0.06` but the text reports `10.99±0.06`. These are from different experimental conditions:
- 11.23 = standard profiling (16 seq × 512 tok), single seed
- 10.99 = hires profiling (64 seq × 2048 tok), mean across 3 seeds, ±0.06 std

The ±0.06 in the table is incorrect. If you're reporting the standard profiling result (11.23), the error bar should come from standard-profiling multi-seed runs, not hires multi-seed runs.

Or maybe the ±0.06 is meant to apply the multi-seed error bar to the single-seed standard result, but that's statistically problematic — you can't mix error bars from different experimental conditions. Looking at the actual multi-seed data, the individual runs range from 10.90 to 11.05, so none of them match the 11.23 value in the table.

```
- mckp_seed0: 11.0168
- mckp_seed1: 10.8981
- mckp_seed2: 11.0487
- mckp_mean: 10.9878
- mckp_std: 0.0648
```

The 11.23 must be coming from standard profiling instead. Now I'm checking whether the multi-seed experiment actually used hires or standard profiling — the description mentions 3 different calibration seeds but doesn't specify which profiling method. Given that the seed results cluster around 10.90-11.05, that's much closer to the hires value of 10.91 than the standard 11.23, so it likely used hires. And the paper itself states the mean as 10.99±0.06, which matches the multi-seed hires results.

So the table is mixing data from two different profiling setups: the point estimate comes from standard profiling while the error bar comes from hires multi-seed. That's a genuine data integrity problem. I need to either report the hires multi-seed result consistently as 10.99±0.06, or report just the standard profiling value without the error bar.

But wait—the paper explicitly states it's reporting the standard profiling result as the headline. So the ±0.06 shouldn't be attached to 11.23 at all since those seeds came from hires profiling. Let me verify whether there was actually a standard profiling multi-seed experiment by looking at the individual seed values. They're all clustering around 10.99, which is much closer to the hires result of 10.91 than to the standard profiling value of 11.23. That suggests these seeds might not be from standard profiling at all.

The appendix confirms it: under hires profiling, the mean is 10.99 ± 0.06. So Table 1 is mixing the standard profiling PPL (11.23) with error bars from hires profiling (±0.06)—that's the inconsistency.

I'm also noticing the unconstrained columns for Llama, Mistral, Yi, and Gemma are all blank because of the 2bitonly profile issue that prevented W2 from completing those runs.

So the key issues are: the error bar mismatch in Table 1 needs fixing—either report 10.99±0.06 or drop the error bar from 11.23—and the "Don't Waste Bits!" citation that the reviewer flagged is still missing.

The provenance file is outdated with old model versions, and W2/W9 are still waiting to execute with GPUs sitting idle. Let me focus the report to the Director on the actionable problems: the mixing issue and the citation.

Now I'm checking whether the multi-seed error bars experiment actually has high resolution data by looking at the full registry entry.
````

2-bit 是人为产物，不是 solver 真实行为。Qwen 用 full profile 只有 60% 2-bit。\n2. **W9 的 ratio 300-500× 听起来很强但是 Layer-0 饱和伪信号**——n=4 刚好是 Layer 0 全部 4 个 KV head，这是层级灾难不是渐进 compounding。有价值的只有 n=1 (ratio=1.0) 和 n=2 (ratio=3.84)。\n3. **\"2bitonly\" profiles 与 \"full\" profiles 的区别**：2bitonly 只测了 d_h(2)，没有 d_h(4)/d_h(8)。full profile 测了所有 bit-width。Qwen 的已有数据（Table 4/Fig 2）都基于 full profile。用 2bitonly 跑 solver 会让 4-bit/8-bit distortion = 0，solver 被误导。\n4. **Table 1 的 11.23±0.06 混用了 standard profiling PPL 和 hires profiling error bar**——没有 standard profiling 的多种子数据。\n5. **\"Don't Waste Bits!\" 引用**被 W7 worker 跳过，reviewer 原文提了这篇。\n\n## 项目硬约束 (memory.md 节选)\n\n## 约束\n\n- westd-37397 GPU 白名单：cuda:0, cuda:1（两张 RTX PRO 6000 96GB）\n- 论文值锁定：Qwen concentration=0.61 (float32, commit bfaea4e), Mistral concentration=0.24/t1t5=0.41 (hires, commit e52fb0e)\n- Flat 分类诊断条件：concentration ≥0.50 AND t1/t5 ≤0.70 → 5/5 正确分类\n\n## 可查的历史\n前 7 轮 handoff 在 `task_context/history/reviewer/loop_*.md`，如有疑问可 Read 回查。\n完整聊天归档可用 `recall_session_history(\"关键词\")` 搜索。\n\n## 第一个动作要求\n1. 用 `read_registry` 确认当前 RUNNING/PREPARING 实验状态（防止幻觉记忆过时状态）\n2. 回复一句话确认你看到了上面的硬约束 + 当前活跃实验，再开始任何工具调用"
```json
}
```
